<script>
  function togglePasswordVisibility() {
    const passwordInput = document.getElementById('password');
    const eyeIcon = document.getElementById('passwordEyeIcon');
    if (!passwordInput || !eyeIcon) return;

    if (passwordInput.type === 'password') {
      passwordInput.type = 'text';
      eyeIcon.textContent = 'visibility_off';
    } else {
      passwordInput.type = 'password';
      eyeIcon.textContent = 'visibility';
    }
  }

  function handleLogin() {
    const email = document.getElementById('email').value;
    const password = document.getElementById('password').value;
    const btn = document.getElementById('loginSubmitBtn');
    
    if (!email || !password) return;

    // Feedback visual interactivo en botón
    const originalText = btn.innerHTML;
    btn.innerHTML = `<span class="material-symbols-outlined animate-spin text-[20px]">progress_activity</span><span>Validando credenciales...</span>`;
    btn.disabled = true;

    setTimeout(() => {
      btn.innerHTML = `<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]">check_circle</span><span>¡Acceso Concedido! Redirigiendo...</span>`;
      btn.classList.remove('bg-primary');
      btn.classList.add('bg-secondary');
      setTimeout(() => {
        btn.innerHTML = originalText;
        btn.disabled = false;
        btn.classList.add('bg-primary');
        btn.classList.remove('bg-secondary');
      }, 1800);
    }, 1200);
  }

  function handleForgotPassword() {
    const email = document.getElementById('email').value;
    const promptMsg = email 
      ? `Se ha enviado el enlace de restablecimiento a: ${email}` 
      : 'Por favor, ingresa tu correo @tiendasmass.pe para recuperar tu clave.';
    alert(promptMsg);
  }

  function handleGoogleLogin() {
    alert('Conectando con el directorio corporativo InRetail Google Workspace SSO...');
  }
</script></main></body></html>

<!-- Micro-interactions Script for real-time Gondola Price Tag Updates -->
<script>
    (function() {
      const nameInput = document.getElementById('prod-name');
      const priceMassInput = document.getElementById('price-mass-input');
      const priceMarketInput = document.getElementById('price-market');

      const previewTagTitle = document.getElementById('preview-tag-title');
      const previewTagPrice = document.getElementById('preview-tag-price');
      const previewTagMarket = document.getElementById('preview-tag-market');
      const previewTagSaving = document.getElementById('preview-tag-saving');
      const previewTagKilo = document.getElementById('preview-tag-kilo');

      function updateGondolaTag() {
        if (nameInput && previewTagTitle) {
          previewTagTitle.textContent = nameInput.value.toUpperCase() || 'PRODUCTO SIN NOMBRE';
        }

        const massPriceVal = parseFloat(priceMassInput.value) || 0;
        const marketPriceVal = parseFloat(priceMarketInput.value) || 0;

        if (previewTagPrice) {
          previewTagPrice.textContent = massPriceVal.toFixed(2);
        }

        if (previewTagMarket) {
          previewTagMarket.textContent = 'S/ ' + marketPriceVal.toFixed(2);
        }

        const diff = marketPriceVal - massPriceVal;
        if (previewTagSaving) {
          if (diff > 0) {
            previewTagSaving.textContent = 'AHORRAS S/ ' + diff.toFixed(2);
            previewTagSaving.style.display = 'inline-block';
          } else {
            previewTagSaving.style.display = 'none';
          }
        }

        // Calculate price per kilo (5kg packaging)
        if (previewTagKilo && massPriceVal > 0) {
          const perKilo = (massPriceVal / 5).toFixed(2);
          previewTagKilo.textContent = 'S/ ' + perKilo + ' x Kg';
        }
      }

      if (nameInput) nameInput.addEventListener('input', updateGondolaTag);
      if (priceMassInput) priceMassInput.addEventListener('input', updateGondolaTag);
      if (priceMarketInput) priceMarketInput.addEventListener('input', updateGondolaTag);
    })();
  </script>
</div></main></div>

</body></html>

<script>
  // Batch selection handling for dense retail UX
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
    const table = document.querySelector('table');
    const masterCheckbox = table ? table.querySelector('thead input[type="checkbox"]') : null;
    const rowCheckboxes = table ? table.querySelectorAll('tbody input[type="checkbox"]') : [];
    const batchBar = document.getElementById('batch-action-bar');
    const batchCount = document.getElementById('batch-count');

    function updateBatchState() {
      const checkedBoxes = Array.from(rowCheckboxes).filter(cb => cb.checked);
      const count = checkedBoxes.length;

      if (batchCount) batchCount.textContent = count;

      if (count > 0 && batchBar) {
        batchBar.classList.remove('translate-y-24');
      } else if (batchBar) {
        batchBar.classList.add('translate-y-24');
      }

      if (masterCheckbox) {
        masterCheckbox.checked = count === rowCheckboxes.length && count > 0;
        masterCheckbox.indeterminate = count > 0 && count < rowCheckboxes.length;
      }
    }

    if (masterCheckbox) {
      masterCheckbox.addEventListener('change', () => {
        rowCheckboxes.forEach(cb => cb.checked = masterCheckbox.checked);
        updateBatchState();
      });
    }

    rowCheckboxes.forEach(cb => {
      cb.addEventListener('change', updateBatchState);
    });
  });
</script></main></div></body></html>

<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]">close</span>

<!-- Inline JavaScript for Pure Micro-interactions -->
<script>
  function showFeedback(title, msg) {
    const toast = document.getElementById('actionToast');
    const toastTitle = document.getElementById('toastTitle');
    const toastMsg = document.getElementById('toastMessage');
    
    toastTitle.textContent = title;
    toastMsg.textContent = msg;

    toast.classList.remove('translate-y-24', 'opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
    toast.classList.add('translate-y-0', 'opacity-100');

    setTimeout(() => {
      toast.classList.add('translate-y-24', 'opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
      toast.classList.remove('translate-y-0', 'opacity-100');
    }, 3200);
  }

  function updateFloatingBar() {
    const checkboxes = document.querySelectorAll('.row-checkbox:checked');
    const bar = document.getElementById('floatingActionBar');
    const badge = document.getElementById('selectedCountBadge');

    if (checkboxes.length > 0) {
      badge.textContent = checkboxes.length;
      bar.classList.remove('translate-y-32', 'opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
      bar.classList.add('translate-y-0', 'opacity-100', 'pointer-events-auto');
    } else {
      bar.classList.add('translate-y-32', 'opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
      bar.classList.remove('translate-y-0', 'opacity-100', 'pointer-events-auto');
      const selectAll = document.getElementById('selectAllCheckbox');
      if (selectAll) selectAll.checked = false;
    }
  }

  function toggleSelectAll(master) {
    const checkboxes = document.querySelectorAll('.row-checkbox');
    checkboxes.forEach(cb => {
      // only toggle visible rows
      if (cb.closest('tr').style.display !== 'none') {
        cb.checked = master.checked;
      }
    });
    updateFloatingBar();
  }

  function clearAllSelections() {
    const checkboxes = document.querySelectorAll('.row-checkbox');
    checkboxes.forEach(cb => cb.checked = false);
    const selectAll = document.getElementById('selectAllCheckbox');
    if (selectAll) selectAll.checked = false;
    updateFloatingBar();
  }

  function bulkPrintSelectedTags() {
    const count = document.querySelectorAll('.row-checkbox:checked').length;
    showFeedback('Generando Flejes', `Se enviaron ${count} flejes amarillos a la impresora térmica.`);
    clearAllSelections();
  }

  function syncPOSSelected() {
    const count = document.querySelectorAll('.row-checkbox:checked').length;
    showFeedback('Cajas Sincronizadas', `Precios de ${count} SKUs actualizados en 420 tiendas.`);
    clearAllSelections();
  }

  function applyBulkDiscount() {
    const count = document.querySelectorAll('.row-checkbox:checked').length;
    showFeedback('Ajuste de Margen', `Aplicando matriz de elasticidad a ${count} productos...`);
  }

  // Modal logic
  let activeModalCost = 0;
  function openEditModal(name, price, cost) {
    const modal = document.getElementById('priceEditModal');
    const modalBox = document.getElementById('modalBox');
    
    document.getElementById('modalProductName').textContent = name;
    document.getElementById('modalPriceInput').value = price;
    document.getElementById('modalCostInput').value = 'S/ ' + cost;
    activeModalCost = parseFloat(cost);

    calculateModalMargin();

    modal.classList.remove('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
    modal.classList.add('opacity-100', 'pointer-events-auto');
    modalBox.classList.remove('scale-95');
    modalBox.classList.add('scale-100');
  }

  function closeEditModal() {
    const modal = document.getElementById('priceEditModal');
    const modalBox = document.getElementById('modalBox');
    modal.classList.add('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
    modal.classList.remove('opacity-100', 'pointer-events-auto');
    modalBox.classList.add('scale-95');
    modalBox.classList.remove('scale-100');
  }

  function calculateModalMargin() {
    const priceVal = parseFloat(document.getElementById('modalPriceInput').value);
    const label = document.getElementById('calculatedMargin');
    if (!isNaN(priceVal) && priceVal > activeModalCost) {
      const margin = (((priceVal - activeModalCost) / priceVal) * 100).toFixed(1);
      label.textContent = margin + '%';
      if (margin < 12) {
        label.className = 'font-headline-sm text-headline-sm text-error font-bold';
      } else {
        label.className = 'font-headline-sm text-headline-sm text-[#15803D] font-bold';
      }
    } else {
      label.textContent = '--';
    }
  }

  function saveNewPrice() {
    const newPrice = document.getElementById('modalPriceInput').value;
    const name = document.getElementById('modalProductName').textContent;
    closeEditModal();
    showFeedback('Precio Guardado', `${name} fijado en S/ ${newPrice}. Fleje amarillo puesto en cola.`);
    
    // Live update shelf tag preview
    document.getElementById('previewTagTitle').textContent = name.toUpperCase();
    document.getElementById('previewTagPrice').textContent = parseFloat(newPrice).toFixed(2);
  }

  // Client-side quick filter
  function filtrarTabla() {
    const query = document.getElementById('searchInput').value.toLowerCase();
    const cat = document.getElementById('categoriaFilter').value;
    const brand = document.getElementById('marcaFilter').value;
    const state = document.getElementById('estadoFilter').value;

    const rows = document.querySelectorAll('#tableBody tr');
    rows.forEach(row => {
      const text = row.innerText.toLowerCase();
      const rowCat = row.getAttribute('data-category');
      const rowBrand = row.getAttribute('data-brand');
      const rowState = row.getAttribute('data-state');

      const matchesText = text.includes(query);
      const matchesCat = !cat || rowCat === cat;
      const matchesBrand = !brand || rowBrand === brand;
      const matchesState = !state || rowState === state;

      if (matchesText && matchesCat && matchesBrand && matchesState) {
        row.style.display = '';
      } else {
        row.style.display = 'none';
      }
    });
    updateFloatingBar();
  }

  function setFilterQuick(word) {
    document.getElementById('searchInput').value = word;
    filtrarTabla();
  }

  function resetFilters() {
    document.getElementById('searchInput').value = '';
    document.getElementById('categoriaFilter').value = '';
    document.getElementById('marcaFilter').value = '';
    document.getElementById('estadoFilter').value = '';
    filtrarTabla();
  }

  function filtrarAlerta() {
    document.getElementById('estadoFilter').value = 'Critico';
    filtrarTabla();
  }
</script></main></div></body></html>

    const tabButtons = document.querySelectorAll('.no-scrollbar button');

    btn.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      // Don't execute if default prevented
      const rippleText = btn.querySelector('span:not(.material-symbols-outlined)')?.innerText;
      if (rippleText && (rippleText.includes('Imprimir') || rippleText.includes('Editar'))) {

<script>
  (function() {
    // Interacción simple de pestañas de rango
    const tabs = document.querySelectorAll('#time-range-tabs .tab-btn');
    tabs.forEach(btn => {
      btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        tabs.forEach(b => {
          b.classList.remove('bg-surface-container-lowest', 'text-primary', 'shadow-sm', 'font-semibold');
          b.classList.add('text-on-surface-variant');
        });
        btn.classList.add('bg-surface-container-lowest', 'text-primary', 'shadow-sm', 'font-semibold');
        btn.classList.remove('text-on-surface-variant');
      });
    });

    // Micro-interacción Botones "Reponer"
    const replenishButtons = document.querySelectorAll('.btn-reponer');
    const toast = document.getElementById('toast-replenish');
    const toastText = document.getElementById('toast-text');

    replenishButtons.forEach(btn => {
      btn.addEventListener('click', function() {
        const sku = this.getAttribute('data-sku') || 'SKU';
        this.innerHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[15px] animate-spin">sync</span> Pedido';
        this.classList.replace('bg-secondary-container', 'bg-surface-container-high');
        
        if (toast && toastText) {
          toastText.textContent = `Orden de reposición creada para ${sku}`;
          toast.classList.remove('translate-y-32', 'opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
          setTimeout(() => {
            toast.classList.add('translate-y-32', 'opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
          }, 2400);
        }
      });
    });

    // Botón de reporte rápido
    const exportBtn = document.getElementById('btn-export-report');
    if (exportBtn) {
      exportBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        exportBtn.classList.add('scale-90');
        setTimeout(() => exportBtn.classList.remove('scale-90'), 150);
        if (toast && toastText) {
          toastText.textContent = 'Generando Excel Operativo Tienda #412...';
          toast.classList.remove('translate-y-32', 'opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
          setTimeout(() => {
            toast.classList.add('translate-y-32', 'opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
          }, 2000);
        }
      });
    }
  })();

<!-- Floating Multi-Selection Action Bar (Sticky above bottom nav) -->
<div class="fixed bottom-20 left-4 right-4 z-40 bg-inverse-surface text-inverse-on-surface p-3 rounded-2xl shadow-2xl flex items-center justify-between gap-2 transform transition-all duration-300 translate-y-32 opacity-0 pointer-events-none" id="floating-actions">
<div class="flex items-center gap-2 pl-1 min-w-0">
<div class="w-7 h-7 rounded-full bg-secondary-container text-on-secondary-container flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm font-black" id="selected-counter">0</span>
</div>
<span class="font-label-md text-label-md truncate">Seleccionados</span>
</div>
<div class="flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0">
<button class="flex items-center gap-1 px-3 py-2 bg-secondary-container text-on-secondary-container rounded-xl font-label-md text-label-md font-bold shadow-md active:scale-95 transition-transform" onclick="triggerLabelPrint()" type="button">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px]">print</span>
<span>Imprimir Fleje</span>
</button>
<button aria-label="Actualizar lote" class="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-inverse-surface flex items-center justify-center text-inverse-on-surface active:scale-95 transition-transform" type="button">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]">sync</span>
</button>
<button aria-label="Deseleccionar todos" class="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-inverse-surface/80 flex items-center justify-center text-inverse-on-surface active:scale-95" onclick="clearSelection()" type="button">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px]">close</span>
</button>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<script>
  let selectedCount = 0;

  function toggleItemSelection(button) {
    const card = button.closest('.product-card');
    const icon = button.querySelector('.select-icon');
    const isSelected = card.classList.toggle('selected-item');

    if (isSelected) {
      card.classList.add('bg-surface-container-high');
      card.classList.remove('bg-surface-container-lowest');
      icon.textContent = 'check_box';
      icon.style.fontVariationSettings = "'FILL' 1";
      button.classList.add('bg-primary-container', 'text-on-primary');
      button.classList.remove('bg-surface-container-lowest', 'text-primary-container');
      selectedCount++;
    } else {
      card.classList.remove('bg-surface-container-high');
      card.classList.add('bg-surface-container-lowest');
      icon.textContent = 'check_box_outline_blank';
      icon.style.fontVariationSettings = "'FILL' 0";
      button.classList.remove('bg-primary-container', 'text-on-primary');
      button.classList.add('bg-surface-container-lowest', 'text-primary-container');
      selectedCount--;
    }

    updateFloatingBar();
  }

  function updateFloatingBar() {
    const bar = document.getElementById('floating-actions');
    const counter = document.getElementById('selected-counter');
    counter.textContent = selectedCount;

    if (selectedCount > 0) {
      bar.classList.remove('translate-y-32', 'opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
      bar.classList.add('translate-y-0', 'opacity-100', 'pointer-events-auto');
    } else {
      bar.classList.add('translate-y-32', 'opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
      bar.classList.remove('translate-y-0', 'opacity-100', 'pointer-events-auto');
    }
  }

  function clearSelection() {
    document.querySelectorAll('.product-card.selected-item').forEach(card => {
      const btn = card.querySelector('.item-checkbox');
      if (btn) toggleItemSelection(btn);
    });
    selectedCount = 0;
    updateFloatingBar();
  }

  function triggerLabelPrint() {
    const bar = document.getElementById('floating-actions');
    const originalText = bar.innerHTML;
    // Brief visual confirmation feedback
    const toast = document.createElement('div');
    toast.className = 'fixed top-20 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 bg-primary-container text-on-primary px-4 py-2 rounded-full shadow-lg font-label-md text-label-md z-50 flex items-center gap-2';
    toast.innerHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px] text-secondary-container">check_circle</span> Flejes enviados a cola de impresión';
    document.body.appendChild(toast);
    setTimeout(() => toast.remove(), 2500);
  }

<script>
  function togglePasswordVisibility() {
    const passwordInput = document.getElementById('corporate-password');
    const toggleIcon = document.getElementById('password-toggle-icon');
    
    if (passwordInput.type === 'password') {
      passwordInput.type = 'text';
      toggleIcon.textContent = 'visibility_off';
    } else {
      passwordInput.type = 'password';
      toggleIcon.textContent = 'visibility';
    }
  }

  function handleLoginSubmit() {
    const email = document.getElementById('corporate-email').value;
    const btn = document.getElementById('btn-login-submit');
    const originalText = btn.innerHTML;
    
    // Quick interactive feedback
    btn.disabled = true;
    btn.innerHTML = `
      <span class="material-symbols-outlined animate-spin text-[20px]">progress_activity</span>
      <span>Validando Credenciales...</span>
    `;

    setTimeout(() => {
      btn.disabled = false;
      btn.innerHTML = originalText;
      alert(`Sesión validada exitosamente para ${email}. Redirigiendo a Tienda Mass Próceres #T-0348...`);
    }, 1100);
  }

  function simulateGoogleSso() {
    alert("Iniciando federación de identidad corporativa Single Sign-On (Google Workspace InRetail)...");
  }
</script></main></body></html>

<script>
  (function initProductEditor() {
    const regularInput = document.getElementById('input-price-regular');
    const massInput = document.getElementById('input-price-mass');
    const previewMass = document.getElementById('preview-price-mass');
    const previewRegular = document.getElementById('preview-price-regular');
    const previewSavings = document.getElementById('preview-tag-savings');
    const savingsText = document.getElementById('savings-text');
    const prodNameInput = document.getElementById('prod-name');
    const previewTagName = document.getElementById('preview-tag-name');
    const btnSave = document.getElementById('btn-save');
    const toast = document.getElementById('toast-success');

    function calculateSavings() {
      const reg = parseFloat(regularInput.value) || 0;
      const mass = parseFloat(massInput.value) || 0;

      if (reg > 0 && mass > 0 && reg > mass) {
        const diff = reg - mass;
        const percent = ((diff / reg) * 100).toFixed(1);
        previewMass.textContent = mass.toFixed(2);
        previewRegular.textContent = 'S/ ' + reg.toFixed(2);
        previewSavings.textContent = '-' + percent + '%';
        savingsText.textContent = 'Ahorro: ' + percent + '% (S/ ' + diff.toFixed(2) + ' más barato)';
      } else {
        previewMass.textContent = mass.toFixed(2);
        previewRegular.textContent = 'S/ ' + reg.toFixed(2);
        previewSavings.textContent = 'MASS';
        savingsText.textContent = 'Precio sin ahorro configurado';
      }
    }

    if (regularInput && massInput) {
      regularInput.addEventListener('input', calculateSavings);
      massInput.addEventListener('input', calculateSavings);
    }

    if (prodNameInput && previewTagName) {
      prodNameInput.addEventListener('input', (e) => {
        const val = e.target.value.trim();
        previewTagName.textContent = val ? val.slice(0, 22) : 'PRODUCTO MASS';
      });
    }

    if (btnSave && toast) {
      btnSave.addEventListener('click', () => {
        toast.classList.remove('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none', '-translate-y-4');
        toast.classList.add('opacity-100', 'translate-y-0');
        setTimeout(() => {
          toast.classList.add('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none', '-translate-y-4');
          toast.classList.remove('opacity-100', 'translate-y-0');
        }, 3200);
      });
    }
  })();
</script></main></body></html>

<script>
  function showToast(text, icon = 'check_circle') {
    const toast = document.getElementById('toast-message');
    const toastText = document.getElementById('toast-text');
    const toastIcon = document.getElementById('toast-icon');
    if (!toast) return;

    toastText.innerText = text;
    toastIcon.innerText = icon;
    toast.classList.remove('opacity-0', 'translate-y-3');
    toast.classList.add('opacity-100', 'translate-y-0');

    setTimeout(() => {
      toast.classList.add('opacity-0', 'translate-y-3');
      toast.classList.remove('opacity-100', 'translate-y-0');
    }, 2400);
  }

  function triggerNewOrderToast() {
    showToast('Abriendo Asistente de Reabastecimiento CEDI', 'add_shopping_cart');
  }

  function triggerExportToast() {
    showToast('Generando reporte Excel de despachos...', 'download');
  }

  function openOrderDetail(pedidoId) {
    showToast(`Cargando detalle de ${pedidoId}...`, 'inventory_2');
  }

  function openLiveTracking(pedidoId) {
    showToast(`Conectando con GPS del camión (${pedidoId})`, 'fmd_good');
  }

  function openResolveModal(pedidoId) {
    showToast(`Abriendo acta de discrepancia para ${pedidoId}`, 'edit_document');
  }

  // Filtrado de pestañas
  const tabs = document.querySelectorAll('.tab-btn');
  tabs.forEach(tab => {
    tab.addEventListener('click', () => {
      tabs.forEach(t => {
        t.classList.remove('bg-primary', 'text-on-primary');
        t.classList.add('bg-surface-container-lowest', 'text-on-surface');
      });
      tab.classList.remove('bg-surface-container-lowest', 'text-on-surface');
      tab.classList.add('bg-primary', 'text-on-primary');
      
      const filter = tab.getAttribute('data-filter');
      showToast(`Filtrando pedidos: ${tab.innerText.trim().split('\n')[0]}`, 'filter_list');
    });
  });
</script></main><nav class="fixed bottom-0 w-full z-50 pb-safe bg-surface/90 backdrop-blur-xl shadow-[0_-2px_12px_rgba(11,28,48,0.06)]" data-active-classes="text-primary font-semibold [&amp;_span.material-symbols-outlined]:font-variation-fill"><div class="flex justify-around items-center h-16 px-gutter-compact"><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-0.5
